## 散步摔倒后请同伴联系急救员 · p04-a-01

主题：健康与照护；情景：摔倒后寻求帮助（walking-fall-assistance）。

沟通目标：说明不适并请同伴联系现场急救员

背景：散步者跌倒后向同行朋友说明情况；对话只练习现场求助沟通。

人物：Rosa、Ben。

起草：author-phase04-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-a-01-s01 · Rosa: I slipped on that step and hurt my knee.
- p04-a-01-s02 · Ben: Can you tell me where the pain is?
- p04-a-01-s03 · Rosa: Just here. I would rather not try to walk yet.
- p04-a-01-s04 · Ben: There is a first-aid tent by the gate. I will call them immediately.
- p04-a-01-s05 · Rosa: Please tell them about the knee injury and ask them to come here.
- p04-a-01-s06 · Ben: I will. You do not have to go looking for medical help on your own.

目标用法：

- hurt · v. · 使身体受伤；义项 sense-staged-ox-e0e240b2a66ad016-p04-a-01；来源 ox-e0e240b2a66ad016；证据 p04-a-01-s01；词形 hurt。
- knee · n. · 膝盖；义项 sense-staged-ox-8b0a2973edfb9a81-p04-a-01；来源 ox-8b0a2973edfb9a81；证据 p04-a-01-s01；词形 knee。
- immediately · adv. · 立即；义项 sense-staged-ox-fae24935014cf3b1-p04-a-01；来源 ox-fae24935014cf3b1；证据 p04-a-01-s04；词形 immediately。
- injury · n. · 身体损伤；义项 sense-staged-ox-ba9f89f901c931ce-p04-a-01；来源 ox-ba9f89f901c931ce；证据 p04-a-01-s05；词形 injury。
- medical · adj. · 医疗的；义项 sense-staged-ox-c286bf3f535d4b8a-p04-a-01；来源 ox-c286bf3f535d4b8a；证据 p04-a-01-s06；词形 medical。

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The fall, localized knee pain, request to avoid walking, and arrangement for first-aid staff to come to Rosa form one coherent help-seeking exchange. The title and goal promise a request for assistance, not a diagnosis or completed treatment, and Ben's final assurance closes that request.

- ox-8b0a2973edfb9a81：accepted；In s01, 'my knee' names the injured body part; the later request for staff to attend the knee injury keeps the noun's reference unambiguous.（p04-a-01-s01）。
- ox-fae24935014cf3b1：accepted；In s04, the adverb modifies 'will call' and means without delay, naturally emphasizing the response to Rosa's fall.（p04-a-01-s04）。
- ox-ba9f89f901c931ce：accepted；In s05, 'the knee injury' is a noun phrase naming the physical damage already described, rather than emotional hurt or a general illness.（p04-a-01-s05）。

## 生病请假并确认今天的替班 · p04-a-02

主题：工作与协作；情景：协调病假期间的班次交接（sick-day-shift-cover）。

沟通目标：确认替班人员及常规工作的交接内容

背景：员工因病致电同事，确认日常任务由谁接手。

人物：Yasmin、Owen。

起草：author-phase04-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-a-02-s01 · Yasmin: I am too ill to come in today. Can you manage without me?
- p04-a-02-s02 · Owen: Yes. I can cover the front desk, and Priya has offered to do the deliveries.
- p04-a-02-s03 · Yasmin: Thanks. I normally print the delivery list before nine.
- p04-a-02-s04 · Owen: I will print it now. Is the latest copy in the shared folder?
- p04-a-02-s05 · Yasmin: Yes, under today’s date. Should I choose illness as the reason on the absence form?
- p04-a-02-s06 · Owen: Yes. I will also tell the afternoon manager that you are away.
- p04-a-02-s07 · Yasmin: Good, that is everything for this morning. Thank you for covering.

目标用法：

- ill · adj. · 生病的；义项 sense-staged-ox-7784f3101efc25be-p04-a-02；来源 ox-7784f3101efc25be；证据 p04-a-02-s01；词形 ill。
- manage · v. · 设法应付；义项 sense-staged-ox-44e1adea0925c33f-p04-a-02；来源 ox-44e1adea0925c33f；证据 p04-a-02-s01；词形 manage。
- normally · adv. · 通常；义项 sense-staged-ox-1fb108557035217f-p04-a-02；来源 ox-1fb108557035217f；证据 p04-a-02-s03；词形 normally。
- illness · n. · 疾病；生病；义项 sense-staged-ox-ed7ca9d2e0075f73-p04-a-02；来源 ox-ed7ca9d2e0075f73；证据 p04-a-02-s05；词形 illness。
- manager · n. · 经理；负责人；义项 sense-staged-ox-ffd098c6ad686f45-p04-a-02；来源 ox-ffd098c6ad686f45；证据 p04-a-02-s06；词形 manager。

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Yasmin's illness motivates a practical work handover: Owen covers reception, Priya handles deliveries, the delivery list is located, and the absence form and afternoon manager are addressed. The closing thanks confirms the morning handover rather than claiming the illness has resolved.

- ox-44e1adea0925c33f：accepted；In s01, 'manage without me' is an intransitive verb meaning cope; the proposed staffing arrangements answer that question directly.（p04-a-02-s01）。
- ox-1fb108557035217f：accepted；In s03, the adverb places printing the delivery list within Yasmin's usual routine, supplying the handover information Owen needs.（p04-a-02-s03）。

## 预约颈部不适检查 · p04-a-03

主题：健康与照护；情景：描述问题并预约就诊（routine-clinic-booking）。

沟通目标：准确说明不适位置并确认预约时间

背景：患者与诊所接待员核对预约时间及应向医生提供的信息；不讨论诊断或用药调整。

人物：Leila、Receptionist。

起草：author-phase04-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-a-03-s01 · Leila: Could I book an appointment? My neck has been sore since yesterday.
- p04-a-03-s02 · Receptionist: Is there anything you want me to add to the booking note?
- p04-a-03-s03 · Leila: The pain is mild, but it does not feel normal. I also have a question about my medicine.
- p04-a-03-s04 · Receptionist: Please mention that to the doctor and bring the name of the medicine. Can you come at ten tomorrow?
- p04-a-03-s05 · Leila: Yes, ten is fine.
- p04-a-03-s06 · Receptionist: You are booked for ten tomorrow morning with Dr Lee.

目标用法：

- neck · n. · 颈部；义项 sense-staged-ox-5ebe847034dc7d41-p04-a-03；来源 ox-5ebe847034dc7d41；证据 p04-a-03-s01；词形 neck。
- normal · adj. · 正常的；义项 sense-staged-ox-41d69c17663f5103-p04-a-03；来源 ox-41d69c17663f5103；证据 p04-a-03-s03；词形 normal。
- medicine · n. · 药物；义项 sense-staged-ox-094a6b9f9ed27258-p04-a-03；来源 ox-094a6b9f9ed27258；证据 p04-a-03-s03；词形 medicine。
- mention · v. · 提到；向人说明；义项 sense-staged-ox-16a7f34c4f3b420c-p04-a-03；来源 ox-16a7f34c4f3b420c；证据 p04-a-03-s04；词形 mention。

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The patient states the affected area and duration, adds a medication question without receiving medical advice, and explicitly agrees to ten tomorrow. The receptionist confirms the time and clinician, so the booking title and goal are actually fulfilled.

- ox-41d69c17663f5103：accepted；In s03, 'does not feel normal' is a predicative adjective describing departure from the patient's ordinary physical state, not the intensity of pain.（p04-a-03-s03）。
- ox-094a6b9f9ed27258：accepted；In s03, 'my medicine' denotes medication; s04's request to bring its name confirms the substance sense rather than the academic discipline.（p04-a-03-s03）。

## 把飞进屋里的飞蛾放出去 · p04-a-04

主题：自然与环境；情景：让飞入室内的昆虫离开（indoor-insect-release）。

沟通目标：确认室内昆虫的位置并协作打开通道让它离开

背景：两位室友发现一只飞蛾，商量让它离开房间。

人物：Tessa、Hugo。

起草：author-phase04-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-a-04-s01 · Tessa: There is an insect inside the house, just above the curtain.
- p04-a-04-s02 · Hugo: It is a moth. Please do not kill it.
- p04-a-04-s03 · Tessa: I was only going to open the window. Could you move the curtain?
- p04-a-04-s04 · Hugo: Sure. There it goes, out into the garden.
- p04-a-04-s05 · Tessa: Good. I will close the window now.

目标用法：

- insect · n. · 昆虫；义项 sense-staged-ox-3194b9ba3288cbf9-p04-a-04；来源 ox-3194b9ba3288cbf9；证据 p04-a-04-s01；词形 insect。

- kill · v. · 杀死；义项 sense-staged-ox-e252b9e65baf7790-p04-a-04；来源 ox-e252b9e65baf7790；证据 p04-a-04-s02；词形 kill。

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The roommates locate and identify a moth, agree on a nonlethal way out, move the curtain, and observe it leave before closing the window. The title's release outcome and the scenario's coordinated action are both present.

- ox-958e5cb2ba21d6ad：accepted；In s01, 'inside the house' takes a noun-phrase complement, making inside a preposition of interior location rather than a standalone adverb.（p04-a-04-s01）。

## 先借吉他体验再决定购买 · p04-a-05

主题：文娱与运动；情景：咨询初学吉他借用（beginner-guitar-loan）。

沟通目标：先通过借用体验再决定是否购买

背景：喜欢爵士乐的初学者向乐手朋友咨询并借用吉他。

人物：Ellis、Maya。

起草：author-phase04-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-a-05-s01 · Ellis: I love jazz, but I have only ever been a listener. I would like to try playing.
- p04-a-05-s02 · Maya: Have you thought about learning a musical instrument?
- p04-a-05-s03 · Ellis: The guitar, maybe. You are a musician. Do you think it is a good place to start?
- p04-a-05-s04 · Maya: It can be. You do not need to buy one straight away.
- p04-a-05-s05 · Ellis: That helps. I am nervous about spending money before I know if I enjoy it.
- p04-a-05-s06 · Maya: I can lend you my spare guitar for a month. We can try a few chords on Saturday.
- p04-a-05-s07 · Ellis: Thank you. I will try that before I decide whether to buy one.

目标用法：

- jazz · n. · 爵士乐；义项 sense-staged-ox-3a7a8e943e677b39-p04-a-05；来源 ox-3a7a8e943e677b39；证据 p04-a-05-s01；词形 jazz。
- listener · n. · 听众；聆听者；义项 sense-staged-ox-a45d8562d8e53805-p04-a-05；来源 ox-a45d8562d8e53805；证据 p04-a-05-s01；词形 listener。
- musical · adj. · 音乐的；义项 sense-staged-ox-d7aed7ddbb1e09fa-p04-a-05；来源 ox-d7aed7ddbb1e09fa；证据 p04-a-05-s02；词形 musical。
- instrument · n. · 乐器；义项 sense-staged-ox-409d4ef72aa44d91-p04-a-05；来源 ox-409d4ef72aa44d91；证据 p04-a-05-s02；词形 instrument。
- musician · n. · 音乐家；乐手；义项 sense-staged-ox-aa1b564f11934c9b-p04-a-05；来源 ox-aa1b564f11934c9b；证据 p04-a-05-s03；词形 musician。
- nervous · adj. · 紧张不安的；义项 sense-staged-ox-04d5cd48924310b4-p04-a-05；来源 ox-04d5cd48924310b4；证据 p04-a-05-s05；词形 nervous。
- lend · v. · 借给；义项 sense-staged-ox-ddb377dfaa765dba-p04-a-05；来源 ox-ddb377dfaa765dba；证据 p04-a-05-s06；词形 lend。

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Ellis's interest in playing jazz leads to a concrete low-cost experiment: borrowing Maya's spare guitar for a month and trying chords on Saturday. The listener-to-player motivation and spending concern support all the vocabulary, and the final acceptance matches the try-before-buying goal.

## 确认婚礼邀请和轻松的着装要求 · p04-a-06

主题：家庭与人际；情景：确认婚礼邀请安排（wedding-guest-invitation）。

沟通目标：邀请朋友及其伴侣参加婚礼并澄清着装要求

背景：新人向朋友发出小型婚礼邀请，并说明可以携伴参加。

人物：Nadia、Beth。

起草：author-phase04-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-a-06-s01 · Nadia: Sam and I plan to marry in July. We would love you to come.
- p04-a-06-s02 · Beth: What lovely news! Is the wedding here in town?
- p04-a-06-s03 · Nadia: Yes, in the garden behind the old library. I will send you an invitation with the date and address.
- p04-a-06-s04 · Beth: Thank you. Should I wear something very formal?
- p04-a-06-s05 · Nadia: No, it will be quite informal. And we would like to invite Jo as well.
- p04-a-06-s06 · Beth: I will tell him. We would both love to be there.

- marry · v. · 结婚；义项 sense-staged-ox-1a2719d13898b15e-p04-a-06；来源 ox-1a2719d13898b15e；证据 p04-a-06-s01；词形 marry。
- lovely · adj. · 令人愉快的；美好的；义项 sense-staged-ox-0f58946b8786a691-p04-a-06；来源 ox-0f58946b8786a691；证据 p04-a-06-s02；词形 lovely。
- invitation · n. · 邀请函；义项 sense-staged-ox-4f01a81981c1d305-p04-a-06；来源 ox-4f01a81981c1d305；证据 p04-a-06-s03；词形 invitation。
- informal · adj. · 非正式的；义项 sense-staged-ox-cb7dd65dd26f03f7-p04-a-06；来源 ox-cb7dd65dd26f03f7；证据 p04-a-06-s05；词形 informal。
- invite · v. · 邀请；义项 sense-staged-ox-9400f744dc2036d3-p04-a-06；来源 ox-9400f744dc2036d3；证据 p04-a-06-s05；词形 invite。

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The bride-to-be gives a local wedding invitation, promises the written details, answers the dress-code question, and explicitly includes Jo. Beth accepts in principle for both of them, which meets the invitation and informal-attire goals without claiming a finalized RSVP date.

## 排练时给观众的笑声留出时间 · p04-a-07

主题：文娱与运动；情景：调整喜剧台词的音量与停顿（comedy-audibility-rehearsal）。

沟通目标：降低伴奏音量并约定在观众笑声后继续说台词

背景：两位演员排练喜剧，发现背景音乐和过快的接话影响台词。

人物：Callum、Zoe。

起草：author-phase04-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-a-07-s01 · Callum: The music is too loud. I cannot hear the end of your joke.
- p04-a-07-s02 · Zoe: I will turn it down. Should I speak more loudly too?
- p04-a-07-s03 · Callum: A little, but the bigger problem is the timing. Wait for the laughter to stop before your next line.
- p04-a-07-s04 · Zoe: So I should look at you and pause, rather than talk over the audience?
- p04-a-07-s05 · Callum: Exactly. Let us try that bit again with the music lower.
- p04-a-07-s06 · Zoe: Ready. Start from your entrance.

- loud · adj. · 声音大的；义项 sense-staged-ox-e2d1c3cdb0106baa-p04-a-07；来源 ox-e2d1c3cdb0106baa；证据 p04-a-07-s01；词形 loud。

## 向邻居反映深夜电视声 · p04-a-22

- p04-a-22-s04 · Elena: Of course. If I make it less loud, would that help?
- p04-a-22-s05 · Martin: It might, but the wall carries the deeper sounds even at a low volume.
- p04-a-22-s06 · Elena: I understand. I will turn it off now and finish the programme tomorrow.
- p04-a-22-s07 · Martin: Thank you. I appreciate it.

沟通目标：了解桥梁封闭情况并确认替代路线和停车后步行的安排

背景：骑手向封路现场的警员询问路线，并决定停好车后步行到目的地。

- p04-a-27-s01 · Nikhil: Excuse me, officer. Can I take my motorcycle across the bridge?
- p04-a-27-s02 · Officer Price: No, the notice by the barrier explains that it is closed to all traffic today.
- p04-a-27-s03 · Nikhil: Is it likely to open this afternoon? I need to get to the museum.
- p04-a-27-s04 · Officer Price: Not today. Use the north bridge, then turn left into Market Street.
- p04-a-27-s05 · Nikhil: There seems to be nowhere to park by the museum entrance.
- p04-a-27-s06 · Officer Price: There is a public car park at the end of Market Street, with spaces for motorcycles.
- p04-a-27-s07 · Nikhil: Thanks. I will go that way, park there, and walk the last bit.

## 询问住房法律咨询的其他可约时间 · p04-a-28

主题：消费与服务；情景：查询住房法律咨询的可约时间（housing-law-consultation-inquiry）。

- p04-a-28-s04 · Receptionist: You can bring written notes if that helps. We have Tuesday at two or Thursday at eleven.
- p04-a-28-s05 · Daniela: Neither appointment fits my work schedule. Is an evening call possible?
- p04-a-28-s06 · Receptionist: I will ask about evening availability and call you back. What number should I use?
- p04-a-28-s07 · Daniela: Please use the number on my contact form. I will wait for your call.

The tenant identifies the needed legal service, explains a new matter and her wish to communicate calmly, and rejects both offered times because of work. ## 确认孩子能否试演国王角色 · p04-a-29

主题：文娱与运动；情景：确认角色分配和排练（community-play-casting）。

- p04-a-29-s01 · Beatrice: My son wants to try for the king, but he is the youngest kid in the group. Is that a problem?
- p04-a-29-s02 · Amos: No. The role needs a clear voice, not a tall actor.
- p04-a-29-s03 · Beatrice: He will feel lucky just to have a chance. When should he come?
- p04-a-29-s04 · Amos: The audition is on Wednesday at four. If he gets the part, rehearsals will be at the same time each week.
- p04-a-29-s05 · Beatrice: That works for us. He will practise the short speech you sent.
- p04-a-29-s06 · Amos: Good. Wish him luck, and remind him to bring the script on Wednesday.

- king · n. · 国王；义项 sense-staged-ox-b31975dbdda9e480-p04-a-29；来源 ox-b31975dbdda9e480；证据 p04-a-29-s01；词形 king。

- lucky · adj. · 幸运的；义项 sense-staged-ox-a5d47a2f47ca6826-p04-a-29；来源 ox-a5d47a2f47ca6826；证据 p04-a-29-s03；词形 lucky。
- luck · n. · 好运；义项 sense-staged-ox-8ecce3f2bbe2531d-p04-a-29；来源 ox-8ecce3f2bbe2531d；证据 p04-a-29-s06；词形 luck。

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-a；整篇结论：accepted。

A parent checks whether a young child can audition, learns that a clear voice matters, and receives an audition time plus conditional rehearsal timing. Preparation and the script reminder close the exchange naturally. 'Try for the king' is an informal elliptical role reference made clear by 'role', 'audition', and 'part'; no casting success is prematurely claimed.

人物：Omar、Clare。

- p04-a-30-s01 · Omar: My lifestyle has become very inactive since I started working from home.

- p04-a-30-s03 · Omar: Yes. Do I need a fitness watch or anything like that?
- p04-a-30-s04 · Clare: That is not necessary for walking with me. We can just go round the block and see how you like it.
- p04-a-30-s05 · Omar: Let us try tomorrow at half past twelve.
- p04-a-30-s06 · Clare: Great. I will meet you outside your building.

Omar's less-active home-working routine prompts a modest shared walk rather than a medical plan or equipment purchase. They dismiss a fitness watch as unnecessary for this outing and agree on tomorrow at twelve thirty outside his building, fulfilling the small first-step goal.

## 把六人份食谱减半做晚饭 · p04-b-01

主题：饮食沟通；情景：分工准备晚饭（shared-dinner-preparation）。

沟通目标：商定晚餐分工、菜品和份量

背景：露西和奥马尔在家做三人份烤蔬菜晚饭，商定份量并分工。

人物：Lucy、Omar。

起草：author-phase04-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-b-01-s01 · Lucy: Shall we cook dinner ourselves tonight? We have enough vegetables.
- p04-b-01-s02 · Omar: Yes. This recipe looks easy, but the quantity is for six people.
- p04-b-01-s03 · Lucy: Then use half of everything. We only need three portions.
- p04-b-01-s04 · Omar: I will prepare the vegetables. Could you heat the oven?
- p04-b-01-s05 · Lucy: Sure. Do we have anything to serve with them?
- p04-b-01-s06 · Omar: There is some tomato sauce in the refrigerator. We can warm it in a pan.
- p04-b-01-s07 · Lucy: Good. Pass me that plate, please. I need somewhere to put the peelings.
- p04-b-01-s08 · Omar: Here you are. You handle the sauce, and I will get the vegetables ready for the oven.

## 确定筹款海报印量与发布分工 · p04-b-08

- p04-b-08-s07 · Tessa: Good. Once we approve that, I will put the same design on our website.

## 报告屋顶漏水并约定检查时间 · p04-b-09

主题：居家生活；情景：预约屋顶漏水检查（roof-leak-inspection-appointment）。

沟通目标：说明漏水情况及已采取的隔离措施，约定检查时间并在检查后讨论维修选项

背景：屋主致电维修人员，说明已经采取的隔离安排并预约检查。

人物：Gareth、Lina。

起草：author-phase04-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-b-09-s01 · Gareth: I am the owner of the house on Mill Lane. Water is coming through the roof into the spare room.
- p04-b-09-s02 · Lina: Is anyone using that room now?
- p04-b-09-s03 · Gareth: No. An electrician has already turned off the power to it, and we are keeping the door closed.
- p04-b-09-s04 · Lina: Good to know. Has the water reached any furniture?
- p04-b-09-s05 · Gareth: We moved the furniture earlier to protect it. Can you stop more water getting in?
- p04-b-09-s06 · Lina: I need to inspect the roof before I can say what work will prevent another leak. I can come at two today.
- p04-b-09-s07 · Gareth: Two is fine. We will stay out of the room until you have checked it.
- p04-b-09-s08 · Lina: I will inspect it then and explain the repair options before starting work.

The new roof-leak-inspection-appointment scenario and revised title now accurately describe reporting water ingress, prior electrical isolation, a two-o'clock inspection and repair-options discussion afterwards. The speakers explicitly leave the repair choice until inspection, so the former mismatch with a promised repair is resolved without changing the fixed home-repair scenario. The safety-conscious context, furniture question and appointment confirmation form a coherent complete exchange.

- ox-e309091c93343a20：accepted；s05: 'moved the furniture earlier to protect it' explains moving furniture to avoid water damage. The purpose infinitive and object it correctly express the transitive verb 保护, with the furniture as clear antecedent.（p04-b-09-s05）。
- ox-a200c4c9d22e68d3：accepted；s06: 'what work will prevent another leak' asks which future work could stop recurrence. The transitive verb takes another leak as object and correctly means 防止 rather than falsely asserting that the existing leak is repaired.（p04-b-09-s06）。

## 给环境调查报道加上样本限制 · p04-b-10

主题：研究与论证；情景：讨论调查证据（survey-evidence）。

沟通目标：核对样本、局限与结论的依据

背景：记者与研究者核对一项虚构的近期调查，避免把受访者意见写成全城事实。

人物：Lena、Dr Bell。

起草：author-phase04-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-b-10-s01 · Lena: I am the reporter working on your recent survey. Can I say that air pollution worries half the city?
- p04-b-10-s02 · Dr Bell: Not from these results. Forty percent of the people who answered mentioned it, not half.
- p04-b-10-s03 · Lena: And the people who answered are not necessarily like the whole population?
- p04-b-10-s04 · Dr Bell: Exactly. We invited members of two neighbourhood groups. As a researcher, I need to make that limit clear.
- p04-b-10-s05 · Lena: Can the survey predict whether air quality will get worse?
- p04-b-10-s06 · Dr Bell: No. We asked people what worried them. We did not measure the air.
- p04-b-10-s07 · Lena: Then I will describe the respondents and avoid a forecast. Can you check that paragraph before we publish it?
- p04-b-10-s08 · Dr Bell: Yes. Send me the paragraph, and I will check that it matches the findings.

The researcher corrects fifty to forty percent, identifies the restricted two-group sample and distinguishes opinions from measured air quality. The reporter agrees to name respondents, omit forecasts and seek a paragraph check, matching the sample-limit title and evidence-checking goal. No invented real-world findings are asserted outside the fictional survey.

- p04-b-11-s03 · Amal: The speaker says they will meet in the hall. Does the word “they” refer to the musicians or the audience?
- p04-b-11-s04 · Jo: The musicians. She mentions them in the sentence just before that.
- p04-b-11-s05 · Amal: That makes sense. I also need to pronounce the name of the hall when I give the announcement.
- p04-b-11-s06 · Jo: Let us replay the first line. She says the name slowly there, so you can repeat it after her.
- p04-b-11-s07 · Amal: Then can we listen to the two sentences about the musicians together?
- p04-b-11-s08 · Jo: Yes. You are making progress: you have identified exactly which parts need another listen.

Reread all eight turns, metadata and six targets at content_version 2. s03 now asks 'Does the word “they” refer to the musicians or the audience?', with a grammatical singular metalinguistic subject and an explicit pronoun mention. Jo resolves the antecedent, then proposes replaying the hall name and surrounding sentences; Amal accepts and requests the related passage. The listening-clarification title and goal fit this concrete replay plan, and the earlier refer-evidence defect is resolved.

- ox-fa9991550b68d0fa：accepted；s01: 'one particular sentence' narrows Amal's uncertainty to a specific sentence in the recording. The adjective modifies sentence and means 特定的, as Jo's immediate 'Which one?' reinforces.（p04-b-11-s01）。
- ox-2e004916be6312bb：accepted；s03: 'Does the word “they” refer to the musicians or the audience?' now has the singular subject 'the word “they”', correctly agreeing with does. - ox-4883d39f77e2b219：accepted；s05: 'pronounce the name of the hall' means say its sounds correctly for an announcement. The transitive verb takes name as object, and s06's slow model plus repetition supports 发音／读出.（p04-b-11-s05）。

## 凭刻字认领母亲留下的戒指 · p04-b-14

主题：消费与服务；情景：描述并认领遗失戒指（lost-ring-identification）。

沟通目标：凭外观和刻字核实遗失戒指的归属并领取

背景：访客向场馆失物招领处描述遗失戒指，并用细节核实归属。

人物：Hazel、Owen。

起草：author-phase04-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-b-14-s01 · Hazel: Has anyone handed in a silver ring? I put it in my coat pocket during the workshop, and now it is gone.
- p04-b-14-s02 · Owen: We have one. Before I show it to you, can you describe a detail that would help identify it?
- p04-b-14-s03 · Hazel: There is a small blue stone, and the letters M and H are inside. It was my mother’s most treasured possession.
- p04-b-14-s04 · Owen: Those details match. Is this the one?
- p04-b-14-s05 · Hazel: Yes. I recognize the little scratch beside the stone.
- p04-b-14-s06 · Owen: A cleaner found it under a chair. You can sign this collection record and take it.
- p04-b-14-s07 · Hazel: Thank you. I was about to search the whole room again. I will sign here.

- search · v. · 搜索；查找；义项 sense-staged-ox-bfd28f85f8153e41-p04-b-14；来源 ox-bfd28f85f8153e41；证据 p04-b-14-s07；词形 search。

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The visitor supplies the stone and internal letters before viewing the ring, then confirms its scratch and agrees to sign the collection record. This gives a coherent identification-to-return outcome matching the title and ownership-checking goal, with no speaker confusion.

- ox-d8af4e6a10ec2ee1：accepted；s01: 'a silver ring' names the small item of jewellery later identified by its stone and engraved letters, clearly 戒指 rather than a circular arena or sound.（p04-b-14-s01）。
- ox-7b053aec891405ff：accepted；s01: 'my coat pocket' is the clothing compartment where the ring was temporarily placed; the count noun 口袋 naturally explains the loss.（p04-b-14-s01）。
- ox-2963c2266e882ce9：accepted；s03: 'my mother’s most treasured possession' refers to the cherished ring as a thing owned, not the state of ownership. The count noun and personal-item sense are specific.（p04-b-14-s03）。
- ox-f01df1b1078949c7：accepted；s05: 'recognize the little scratch beside the stone' means identify a familiar distinguishing mark, supporting the transitive verb 认出 and the ring claim.（p04-b-14-s05）。

## 先在工作人员陪同下认识一只狗 · p04-b-15

主题：自然与环境；情景：安排初次接触收容所的狗（shelter-dog-first-meeting）。

沟通目标：说明养狗顾虑并同意在工作人员陪同下先短暂见面

背景：一位考虑养宠物的人向收容所说明自己的顾虑，决定先短暂见面。

人物：Jules、Mina。

起草：author-phase04-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-b-15-s01 · Jules: I moved into a house recently, and I am thinking about getting a pet. But I am a little scared of large dogs.
- p04-b-15-s02 · Mina: You do not have to choose one today. What kind of personality would suit you?
- p04-b-15-s03 · Jules: A calm dog. How does this small one react when someone new arrives?
- p04-b-15-s04 · Mina: He usually watches for a while before coming closer. We can meet him together in the quiet room.
- p04-b-15-s05 · Jules: He does seem calm from here. Could you stay with me for the first few minutes?
- p04-b-15-s06 · Mina: Of course. We will start with a short visit, and you can tell me when you want to stop.
- p04-b-15-s07 · Jules: That sounds good. Let us try the short visit before discussing anything else.

- recently · adv. · 最近；义项 sense-staged-ox-941417d5d143beb3-p04-b-15；来源 ox-941417d5d143beb3；证据 p04-b-15-s01；词形 recently。

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The prospective owner explains fear of large dogs and preference for a calm companion. Staff offer a short, accompanied meeting with no pressure to adopt, and the visitor accepts precisely that limited next step, matching both title and goal.

- ox-08749f3d574f4510：accepted；s02: 'What kind of personality would suit you?' asks about the dog's temperament, answered by 'A calm dog'. The noun 性格 fits this common animal-personality use.（p04-b-15-s02）。

## 把拒绝临时加班的回复写得清楚有礼 · p04-b-16

主题：工作与协作；情景：修改拒绝工作请求的措辞（polite-work-request-refusal）。

沟通目标：把拒绝临时加班的草稿改为明确有礼且时间承诺可行的回复

背景：一位员工请同事帮忙检查准备发送的拒绝措辞，尚未发送消息。

人物：Paula、Dion。

起草：author-phase04-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-b-16-s01 · Paula: I need to refuse another request to stay late tonight. Does this reply sound rude?
- p04-b-16-s02 · Dion: Read it to me.
- p04-b-16-s03 · Paula: No, I already said I am busy.
- p04-b-16-s04 · Dion: That sounds irritated. You can be polite without changing your answer: I cannot stay tonight, but I can finish the task tomorrow morning.
- p04-b-16-s05 · Paula: Tomorrow morning is realistic. I will use that and leave out the comment about having said it already.
- p04-b-16-s06 · Dion: Good. It gives a clear answer and tells them when the work can be done.

- refuse · v. · 拒绝；义项 sense-staged-ox-fcb73ef18f19131f-p04-b-16；来源 ox-fcb73ef18f19131f；证据 p04-b-16-s01；词形 refuse。

- rude · adj. · 无礼的；义项 sense-staged-ox-531fef44b88628cf-p04-b-16；来源 ox-531fef44b88628cf；证据 p04-b-16-s01；词形 rude。

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The employee reads an irritated refusal, receives a clearer alternative and explicitly checks that tomorrow morning is realistic before adopting it. The ending identifies the revised answer and feasible timing; it does not claim the message has been sent, consistent with context and goal.

- ox-a5d4529a85c9ac68：accepted；s01: 'this reply' names the response draft which Paula reads in s03. It is a count noun 回复, not the verb to answer.（p04-b-16-s01）。
- ox-531fef44b88628cf：accepted；s01: 'Does this reply sound rude?' predicates discourteous tone of the draft; Dion's reaction to the irritated wording makes 无礼的 contextually apt.（p04-b-16-s01）。

## 决定先听取同事的不满再道歉 · p04-b-17

主题：工作与协作；情景：确定向同事道歉的下一步（colleague-apology-next-step）。

沟通目标：承认打断同事的问题并确定先倾听和可兑现的改进行动

背景：同事之间的讨论受到打断后，一位员工向朋友商量如何修复合作关系。

人物：Kian、Rose。

起草：author-phase04-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-b-17-s01 · Kian: I interrupted Amira twice in the meeting. I did not realize how upset she was until she spoke to me afterwards.
- p04-b-17-s02 · Rose: How did you respond?
- p04-b-17-s03 · Kian: I said I was only trying to help. That made things worse. I care about our working relationship.
- p04-b-17-s04 · Rose: Then start by listening, not by defending yourself. What can you honestly offer to do differently?
- p04-b-17-s05 · Kian: I can promise to let her finish before I ask questions.
- p04-b-17-s06 · Rose: That is specific. Tell her you are sorry, ask what she wanted to say, and give her time to answer.
- p04-b-17-s07 · Kian: I will ask whether she has a few minutes after lunch. I will listen first.

目标用法：

- realize · v. · 意识到；义项 sense-staged-ox-f7208311bd8764a5-p04-b-17；来源 ox-f7208311bd8764a5；证据 p04-b-17-s01；词形 realize。
- respond · v. · 回应；义项 sense-staged-ox-241aab9d48c7836c-p04-b-17；来源 ox-241aab9d48c7836c；证据 p04-b-17-s02；词形 respond。
- relationship · n. · 关系；义项 sense-staged-ox-d5c98f14422ea387-p04-b-17；来源 ox-d5c98f14422ea387；证据 p04-b-17-s03；词形 relationship。
- promise · v. · 承诺；义项 sense-staged-ox-639750e6cb59b934-p04-b-17；来源 ox-639750e6cb59b934；证据 p04-b-17-s05；词形 promise。

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The employee recognises that interrupting and defending himself damaged a working relationship. He identifies the specific change of letting Amira finish and decides to request a brief conversation and listen first. This is a next-step plan rather than a claimed completed reconciliation, matching the title and goal.

- ox-f7208311bd8764a5：accepted；s01: 'did not realize how upset she was until ...' uses the cognition verb with an embedded degree clause, meaning become aware of her distress, not achieve an ambition.（p04-b-17-s01）。
- ox-241aab9d48c7836c：accepted；s02: 'How did you respond?' asks for Kian's verbal reaction to Amira raising the problem; s03 supplies that reply, supporting the intransitive 回应 sense.（p04-b-17-s02）。
- ox-d5c98f14422ea387：accepted；s03: 'our working relationship' names the professional interpersonal connection he wants to preserve; the count noun 关系 is explicitly non-romantic here.（p04-b-17-s03）。
- ox-639750e6cb59b934：accepted；s05: 'promise to let her finish' uses the verb with a to-infinitive for a specific future commitment. The promise sense 承诺 fits the proposed behavioural change.（p04-b-17-s05）。

## 错开医院探视让家人休息 · p04-b-18

主题：健康与照护；情景：协调家人探访安排（family-hospital-visit）。

人物：Nora、Felix。

- p04-b-18-s01 · Nora: Dad has finally fallen asleep. Can we talk quietly out here?
- p04-b-18-s02 · Felix: Of course. Would it be better if I came back later?
- p04-b-18-s03 · Nora: Yes. He needs some peace, and Mum is arriving in a few minutes.
- p04-b-18-s04 · Felix: I will come at four, after Mum leaves. You have been here all morning. Can you rest while she sits with him?
- p04-b-18-s05 · Nora: I will go home for an hour. Thanks for coming later instead of joining us all at once.
- p04-b-18-s06 · Felix: No problem. I will see you at four unless you text me to change it.

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The siblings move their conversation outside, stagger the mother's and Felix's visits, and allow Nora an hour at home. Four o'clock is agreed with a text-based change contingency. This addresses both patient quiet and the companion's rest, matching title and goal without making medical claims.

- ox-ffe5076e8f0f7900：accepted；s03: 'He needs some peace' means freedom from disturbance while Dad sleeps. The mass noun 安静／清静 is supported by avoiding simultaneous visits, not a political peace sense.（p04-b-18-s03）。
- ox-070033c5b11a70ba：accepted；s04: 'Can you rest while she sits with him?' uses rest as an intransitive base verb after can, offering the caregiver a break, confirmed by Nora's plan to go home.（p04-b-18-s04）。

## 核对折后价格并买下外套 · p04-b-19

- p04-b-19-s02 · Finn: Yes. Twenty per cent of fifty pounds is ten pounds, so you pay forty.
- p04-b-19-s03 · Adele: So I save ten pounds. Good. I cannot spend a penny more than forty today.
- p04-b-19-s04 · Finn: Forty is the total. Would you like to try it on before you decide?
- p04-b-19-s05 · Adele: I tried it earlier, and it fits. I will take it. Here is my card.
- p04-b-19-s06 · Finn: Thank you. Here is your receipt. I hope you enjoy wearing it.

## 调整山间步行路线 · p04-c-04

主题：文娱与运动；情景：调整山间步行路线（hill-walk-route-choice）。

沟通目标：考虑天色和路面条件后改走短路线

背景：两名徒步者发现天色转暗，决定放弃山顶景色并沿较短路线返回。

人物：Sana、Ellis。

起草：author-phase04-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-c-04-s01 · Sana: The sky has suddenly gone dark. Do you think a storm is coming?
- p04-c-04-s02 · Ellis: Maybe. We still have a long climb before we get a view of the valley.
- p04-c-04-s03 · Sana: I'd rather miss the view than be up there in bad weather.
- p04-c-04-s04 · Ellis: Agreed. The path towards the village is shorter, and the surface looks firmer than this muddy trail.
- p04-c-04-s05 · Sana: Let's take that path now. We can come back for the hill another day.

- sky · n. · 天空；义项 sense-staged-ox-a87567991a157e4b-p04-c-04；来源 ox-a87567991a157e4b；证据 p04-c-04-s01；词形 sky。

- valley · n. · 山谷；义项 sense-staged-ox-7c1feec1c0886fb2-p04-c-04；来源 ox-7c1feec1c0886fb2；证据 p04-c-04-s02；词形 valley。
- view · n. · 景色；义项 sense-staged-ox-08eff366d84bbfe2-p04-c-04；来源 ox-08eff366d84bbfe2；证据 p04-c-04-s02；词形 view。
- towards · prep. · 朝着；向；义项 sense-staged-ox-d1481c48eee5249d-p04-c-04；来源 ox-d1481c48eee5249d；证据 p04-c-04-s04；词形 towards。
- suddenly · adv. · 突然；义项 sense-staged-ox-3660ec20cf5dbbed-p04-c-04；来源 ox-3660ec20cf5dbbed；证据 p04-c-04-s01；词形 suddenly。

Darkening weather interrupts a climb for a valley view. Both walkers agree on the shorter, firmer village path, so the title, weather/terrain goal and ending agree. The vocabulary expresses the real route trade-off rather than an unrelated outdoor list; towards fits the British wording.

- p04-c-07-s04 · Leah: We can't tell the cause from that alone. I'll record what you've told me for the doctor.

- p04-c-07-s06 · Leah: In this room, please. I'll let the doctor know you're ready.

- p04-c-11-s03 · Priya: Please. My voice keeps dropping out on the high part.
- p04-c-11-s04 · Tomas: We'll try it quietly first, before everyone goes onto the stage.
- p04-c-11-s05 · Priya: That takes some pressure off. You've even got a smile out of me.
- p04-c-11-s06 · Tomas: Good. I'll play the difficult line once, and then you can join me.

## 澄清数学题并完成解题 · p04-c-13

主题：学习与技能；情景：澄清数学题并完成解题（math-problem-support）。

- p04-c-13-s01 · Amy: I feel stupid. I can't solve this problem about twelve students in three equal groups.
- p04-c-13-s02 · Noel: You're not stupid. Show me the step where you get stuck.
- p04-c-13-s03 · Amy: I know there are three groups, but I keep multiplying twelve by three.
- p04-c-13-s04 · Noel: Try a simple drawing. Share the twelve dots between three circles.
- p04-c-13-s05 · Amy: Four in each circle. So the solution is four students in each group, not thirty-six.
- p04-c-13-s06 · Noel: Yes. How would you check your understanding?
- p04-c-13-s07 · Amy: Four plus four plus four makes twelve. Thanks for the support. I'll try the next one myself.

## 核对历史制服展品说明 · p04-c-14

- p04-c-14-s03 · Zoe: What about the silver badge? The one in the photograph looks the same.
- p04-c-14-s04 · Mr Bell: We can describe the matching symbol, but we shouldn't identify the owner from that alone.
- p04-c-14-s05 · Zoe: Then I'll label it as a uniform of the same period and leave the owner's name unknown.
- p04-c-14-s06 · Mr Bell: Good. I'll help you check the museum record before we add anything more.

## 制定小组学习任务目标 · p04-c-23

主题：学习与技能；情景：制定小组学习任务目标（study-task-target-plan）。

沟通目标：把宽泛的学习目标分成可执行任务并约定先试一种策略

背景：两名同学把一周的学习目标拆成短时段，并明确如何判断自己是否真正理解。

人物：Mei、Oscar。

起草：author-phase04-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-c-23-s01 · Mei: What's our target for this week?
- p04-c-23-s02 · Oscar: To finish chapter six, but that's such a big task after work.
- p04-c-23-s03 · Mei: My first thought was to split it over five evenings.
- p04-c-23-s04 · Oscar: I suppose we could, but we both work late on Tuesday. What's your suggestion for that day?
- p04-c-23-s05 · Mei: Let's leave Tuesday free. A better strategy might be three short sessions and one review on Sunday.
- p04-c-23-s06 · Oscar: I like your thinking. What would a successful week look like, apart from getting through the pages?
- p04-c-23-s07 · Mei: If we can each explain the three main ideas without the book, we'll know we've understood them.
- p04-c-23-s08 · Oscar: Then let's take one idea per session. I think we can succeed if we keep each session short.
- p04-c-23-s09 · Mei: Agreed. We'll start with the first idea tonight and test ourselves on Sunday.

## 安排青少年课程活动 · p04-c-24

主题：学习与技能；情景：安排青少年课程活动（teenage-class-planning）。

人物：David、Salma。

- p04-c-24-s01 · David: How is the teaching going with your teenage students?
- p04-c-24-s02 · Salma: Better this term. They talk more when I give them time to work together.
- p04-c-24-s03 · David: What's in the next unit?
- p04-c-24-s04 · Salma: Planning a short radio programme. I was going to choose all the topics.
- p04-c-24-s05 · David: Could they choose the topics themselves? That might give them more reason to speak.
- p04-c-24-s06 · Salma: Yes. I'll give them three broad options, then let each group decide and plan its own programme.

- teaching · n. · 教学；义项 sense-staged-ox-ce3d444fb5db9654-p04-c-24；来源 ox-ce3d444fb5db9654；证据 p04-c-24-s01；词形 teaching。

The teachers use improved peer participation to justify more student control over radio-programme topics. Broad options plus group planning fulfil title and goal of learner independence from teacher selection, not necessarily solo work. Term and programme consistently fit a British educational register.

## 按新喜好挑选生日服饰礼物 · p04-c-25

主题：家庭与人际；情景：按新喜好挑选生日服饰礼物（birthday-outfit-gift）。

- p04-c-25-s03 · Emilia: I was surprised to see him in that bright yellow shirt last week.
- p04-c-25-s04 · Dylan: He loves it. Maybe a casual shirt would be more useful than another tie.
- p04-c-25-s05 · Emilia: Good point. I'll choose a green one in the same style and have it wrapped.
- p04-c-25-s06 · Dylan: Great. I'll check the size on his yellow shirt so you don't have to guess.

The siblings revise an outdated tie idea to a casual shirt after discussing their father's changed clothing habits. Colour, style, wrapping and a size-check handoff create a usable decision matching title and goal, without claiming the gift is already bought. Grey is consistent with the British register.

## 报告被抢走的包 · p04-c-26

- p04-c-26-s01 · Amir: I saw a man steal my bag from the seat next to me. He ran out of the station.
- p04-c-26-s02 · Desk Officer May: Can you describe him and tell me which exit he used?
- p04-c-26-s03 · Amir: The thief had a yellow jacket and went through the doors by the cafe.
- p04-c-26-s04 · Desk Officer May: Did you notice any writing on the jacket?
- p04-c-26-s05 · Amir: Unfortunately, no. It happened so quickly.
- p04-c-26-s06 · Desk Officer May: Stay here with me. I'll contact the police and pass on the description.
- p04-c-26-s07 · Amir: Thank you. I can show you exactly where I was sitting.

## 核实异常的配送延误 · p04-c-27

主题：消费与服务；情景：核实异常的配送延误（service-delay-expectations）。

沟通目标：比较平时配送情况并确认客服下一步查询安排

背景：顾客向客服查询比平时长得多的配送等待，客服确认进一步查询和回复安排。

人物：Nell、Arlo。

起草：author-phase04-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p04-c-27-s01 · Nell: My parcel hasn't moved for a week. Is that a typical wait for this service?
- p04-c-27-s02 · Arlo: No, that's unusual. This route normally takes two working days.
- p04-c-27-s03 · Nell: That's surprising. The tracking page still says it's on schedule.
- p04-c-27-s04 · Arlo: I can see why that's confusing. I'll ask the depot to check where it is and send you an update by tomorrow afternoon.
- p04-c-27-s05 · Nell: Please use the email on my order. I'll wait for that update before arranging another delivery.

目标用法：

- typical · adj. · 通常的；有代表性的；义项 sense-staged-ox-616ffafe54435dba-p04-c-27；来源 ox-616ffafe54435dba；证据 p04-c-27-s01；词形 typical。

- surprising · adj. · 令人惊讶的；义项 sense-staged-ox-55612a1839dc39bd-p04-c-27；来源 ox-55612a1839dc39bd；证据 p04-c-27-s03；词形 surprising。

审阅者：reviewer-phase04-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The agent contrasts the week-long stall with the usual two-day route, acknowledges misleading tracking and promises a depot query and next-afternoon update. The customer provides the reply channel and agrees to wait, matching title and next-step goal without falsely resolving delivery.

## 借工具修好松动支架 · p04-c-28

主题：居家生活；情景：借工具修好松动支架（shelf-bracket-tool-help）。

沟通目标：确认所需工具并约好一起固定架子

- p04-c-28-s01 · Cora: This shelf bracket is loose. Do you have a tool that fits these screws?

- p04-c-28-s03 · Cora: Yes. Any tip for keeping the bracket in place while I tighten it?
- p04-c-28-s04 · Dev: An extra pair of hands would help. I'll hold it while you turn the screwdriver.
- p04-c-28-s05 · Cora: Thanks. Let's do it now, before I put the books back.
- p04-c-28-s06 · Dev: Those screws feel tight. Give the bracket a gentle shake now. Does it still move?

- tool · n. · 工具；义项 sense-staged-ox-45a5c4b38ad190e5-p04-c-28；来源 ox-45a5c4b38ad190e5；证据 p04-c-28-s01；词形 tool。

The screwdriver is identified, the shelf emptied and cooperative holding/tightening roles agreed. A gentle shake then verifies stability, earning the title's completed repair rather than merely promising it. The tool-choice/cooperation goal is met, and returning the screwdriver provides a clear ending.
